{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01Ln85oyowtgxyWmw3wBxjTr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_013ioQNKWybzjaTsxvbfoQAC","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/travis/environment/skills/maven-java-compatibility.md","content":"# Maven Java Version Compatibility\n\n## Overview\nThis skill document covers common Java version compatibility issues in Maven projects, particularly when migrating from Java 8 to Java 9+.\n\n## tools.jar Deprecation (Java 9+)\n\n### The Problem\nPrior to Java 9, the JDK included a `tools.jar` file in `$JAVA_HOME/lib/` that contained:\n- Java compiler API (`com.sun.tools.javac.*`)\n- Annotation processing APIs\n- Other development tools\n\nMany Maven dependencies used a system-scoped dependency like:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### Why It Fails on Java 9+\nIn Java 9, the JDK was modularized (Project Jigsaw). The `tools.jar` was removed and its contents were integrated into:\n- `java.compiler` module\n- `jdk.compiler` module\n\n### Error Message\n```\nCould not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.x.x at specified path /path/to/jdk/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n### Solutions\n\n#### Option 1: Update Dependency Versions\nUpdate libraries that depend on `tools.jar` to newer versions that are Java 9+ compatible.\n\nCommon libraries affected:\n- `com.google.testing.compile:compile-testing` - Use version 0.18+ for Java 9+\n- Some annotation processor libraries\n\n#### Option 2: Add Java 9+ Profile\nAdd a Maven profile that activates on Java 9+ and excludes the problematic dependency:\n\n```xml\n<profile>\n  <id>jdk9+</id>\n  <activation>\n    <jdk>[9,)</jdk>\n  </activation>\n  <dependencies>\n    <dependency>\n      <groupId>problematic-groupId</groupId>\n      <artifactId>problematic-artifactId</artifactId>\n      <exclusions>\n        <exclusion>\n          <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n          <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n        </exclusion>\n      </exclusions>\n    </dependency>\n  </dependencies>\n</profile>\n```\n\n#### Option 3: Exclude Transitive Dependency\nIf the `tools.jar` dependency is coming from a transitive dependency:\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>some.group</groupId>\n  <artifactId>some-artifact</artifactId>\n  <version>x.y.z</version>\n  <exclusions>\n    <exclusion>\n      <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n      <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n    </exclusion>\n  </exclusions>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n## Diagnosing Dependency Issues\n\n### View Dependency Tree\n```bash\nmvn dependency:tree\n```\n\n### Find Specific Dependency Origin\n```bash\nmvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=com.sun:tools\n```\n\n### Analyze Effective POM\n```bash\nmvn help:effective-pom\n```\n\n## compile-testing Library\n\nThe `compile-testing` library from Google is commonly used for testing annotation processors.\n\n### Version History\n- **0.6-0.9**: Requires tools.jar (Java 8 and earlier only)\n- **0.10+**: Works with Java 9+ (removed tools.jar dependency)\n- **0.18+**: Recommended for modern Java versions\n\n### Migration\nUpdate from:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.google.testing.compile</groupId>\n  <artifactId>compile-testing</artifactId>\n  <version>0.7</version>\n  <scope>test</scope>\n</dependency>\n```\n\nTo:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.google.testing.compile</groupId>\n  <artifactId>compile-testing</artifactId>\n  <version>0.18</version>\n  <scope>test</scope>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Avoid system-scoped dependencies**: They are not portable and break across JDK versions\n2. **Use dependency exclusions**: When you can't update a library\n3. **Test with multiple JDK versions**: Use CI/CD to test with Java 8, 11, 17, 21\n4. **Check release notes**: Before upgrading dependencies, check for Java version requirements\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":424,"cache_read_input_tokens":85030,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":424,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5fc11278-6997-4b9d-b2d8-fd3498d36d7d","uuid":"6087573f-bc86-491b-8c46-11cfc131e44a"}

Dependency Resolution Failures\n```\nCould not resolve dependencies for project X: The following artifacts could not be resolved\n```\n\n**Diagnosis:**\n```bash\n# View full dependency tree\nmvn dependency:tree\n\n# Check for specific dependency\nmvn dependency:tree -Dincludes=groupId:artifactId\n\n# Force dependency update\nmvn clean install -U\n```\n\n### 2. Compilation Errors\n```\n[ERROR] Failed to execute goal org.apache.maven.plugins:maven-compiler-plugin\n```\n\n**Diagnosis:**\n```bash\n# Compile with verbose output\nmvn compile -X\n\n# Check effective compiler settings\nmvn help:effective-pom | grep -A 20 \"maven-compiler-plugin\"\n```\n\n### 3. Plugin Execution Failures\n```\n[ERROR] Failed to execute goal groupId:artifactId:version:goal\n```\n\n**Diagnosis:**\n```bash\n# Run with debug output\nmvn clean install -X\n\n# Check plugin configuration\nmvn help:describe -Dplugin=groupId:artifactId -Ddetail\n```\n\n## Debugging Commands\n\n### View Effective POM\nShows the complete POM with all inherited and interpolated values:\n```bash\nmvn help:effective-pom\n```\n\n### View Active Profiles\n```bash\nmvn help:active-profiles\n```\n\n### View System Properties\n```bash\nmvn help:system\n```\n\n### Run Specific Module\n```bash\nmvn compile -pl module-name\nmvn compile -pl module-name -am  # Also make dependencies\n```\n\n## Creating Patches\n\n### Standard Diff Format\nCreate patches using `diff` with unified format:\n```bash\ndiff -u original-file.java modified-file.java > patch.diff\n```\n\n### Git Diff Format\n```bash\ngit diff HEAD -- path/to/file > patch.diff\n```\n\n### Diff Format Anatomy\n```diff\n--- a/path/to/original\n+++ b/path/to/modified\n@@ -start,count +start,count @@\n context line\n-removed line\n+added line\n context line\n```\n\n### Applying Patches\n```bash\n# Dry run (test only)\npatch --dry-run -p1 < patch.diff\n\n# Apply patch\npatch -p1 < patch.diff\n\n# Reverse patch\npatch -R -p1 < patch.diff\n```\n\n## Multi-Module Project Tips\n\n### Build Order Issues\n```bash\n# Build in reactor order\nmvn clean install\n\n# Skip tests for faster iteration\nmvn clean install -DskipTests\n\n# Build single module with dependencies\nmvn clean install -pl module-name -am\n```\n\n### Analyzing Build Order\n```bash\nmvn validate -X 2>&1 | grep \"Reactor Build Order\"\n```\n\n## Common Fixes\n\n### Fixing Missing SNAPSHOT Dependencies\nIf a SNAPSHOT dependency is missing from remote repos but exists locally:\n\n1. Install the local module first:\n```bash\ncd local-module\nmvn clean install -DskipTests\n```\n\n2. Git Diff and Patch Format\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers creating, reading, and applying patches in the standard unified diff format used by Git and GNU diffutils.\n\n## Unified Diff Format\n\n### Basic Structure\n```diff\n--- a/path/to/original.txt\n+++ b/path/to/modified.txt\n@@ -start_line,num_lines +start_line,num_lines @@\n context line (unchanged)\n-removed line\n+added line\n context line (unchanged)\n```\n\n### Header Lines\n- `---`: Original file (prefixed with `a/` in git)\n- `+++`: Modified file (prefixed with `b/` in git)\n\n### Hunk Header\n`@@ -old_start,old_count +new_start,new_count @@`\n- `-old_start,old_count`: Lines in original file\n- `+new_start,new_count`: Lines in modified file\n\n### Content Lines\n- ` ` (space): Context line (unchanged)\n- `-`: Line removed from original\n- `+`: Line added in modified\n- `\\`: No newline at end of file\n\n## Creating Diffs\n\n### Using diff Command\n```bash\n# Basic unified diff\ndiff -u original.txt modified.txt > patch.diff\n\n# With more context lines (default is 3)\ndiff -u -C 5 original.txt modified.txt > patch.diff\n\n# For directories\ndiff -ruN original_dir/ modified_dir/ > patch.diff\n```\n\n### Using Git\n```bash\n# Unstaged changes\ngit diff > patch.diff\n\n# Staged changes\ngit diff --cached > patch.diff\n\n# Specific file\ngit diff -- path/to/file > patch.diff\n\n# Between commits\ngit diff commit1 commit2 > patch.diff\n\n# Create patch file (includes commit info)\ngit format-patch -1 HEAD\n```\n\n## Applying Patches\n\n### Using patch Command\n```bash\n# Apply patch\npatch -p1 < patch.diff\n\n# Dry run (test without applying)\npatch --dry-run -p1 < patch.diff\n\n# Reverse a patch\npatch -R -p1 < patch.diff\n\n# Apply to specific directory\npatch -d /target/directory -p1 < patch.diff\n```\n\n### The -p Option\nThe `-p` option strips leading path components:\n- `-p0`: Use full path as-is\n- `-p1`: Strip first component (e.g., `a/src/file.txt` -> `src/file.txt`)\n- `-p2`: Strip two components\n\n### Using Git Apply\n```bash\n# Apply patch\ngit apply patch.diff\n\n# Check if patch applies\ngit apply --check patch.diff\n\n# Apply with 3-way merge (handles conflicts better)\ngit apply --3way patch.diff\n```\n\n## Example: Complete Diff File\n\n```diff\ndiff --git a/pom.xml b/pom.xml\nindex abc1234..def5678 100644\n--- a/pom.xml\n+++ b/pom.xml\n@@ -45,7 +45,7 @@\n     <dependency>\n       <groupId>com.google.testing.compile</groupId>\n       <artifactId>compile-testing</artifactId>\n-      <version>0.7</version>\n+      <version>0.18</version>\n       <scope>test</scope>\n     </dependency>\n   </dependencies>\n```\n\n## Writing Manual Diffs\n\nWhen creating diffs manually:\n\n1. **Use exact indentation**: Match the original file's whitespace\n2. **Include context lines**: 3 lines before and after changes\n3. **Calculate line numbers accurately**: Count from 1, not 0\n4. **End with newline**: Ensure file ends with newline\n\n### Template\n```diff\n--- a/path/to/file\n+++ b/path/to/file\n@@ -LINE_NUM,CONTEXT_SIZE +LINE_NUM,NEW_SIZE @@\n context before\n-old line\n+new line\n context after\n```\n\n## Common Issues\n\n### Whitespace Problems\n```bash\n# Ignore whitespace\npatch --ignore-whitespace -p1 < patch.diff\n```\n\n### Line Ending Issues\n```bash\n# Convert to Unix line endings\ndos2unix patch.diff\n```\n\n### Fuzz Factor\nWhen patch doesn't match exactly:\n```bash\npatch -p1 --fuzz=3 < patch.diff\n```\n\n## Validating Diffs\n\n### Check Syntax\n```bash\n# Validate diff syntax\ndiffstat patch.diff\n\n# Check if applicable\npatch --dry-run -p1 < patch.diff\n```\n\n### View Changes Summary\n```bash\n# Show file statistics\ndiffstat patch.diff\n\n# List files changed\nlsdiff patch.diff\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always include context**: Makes patches more robust\n2. **Use git format-patch**: For sharing commits (includes metadata)\n3. **Test before applying**: Use `--dry-run` or `--check`\n4. **Use -p1 with git diffs**: Standard convention for git-generated patches\n5. **Version control your patches**: Store them in the repo when needed\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1183,"cache_read_input_tokens":86703,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1183,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5fc11278-6997-4b9d-b2d8-fd3498d36d7d","uuid":"eadce2b7-ccb5-4100-bb9f-83f87b933ff1"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_0171DE6BPQbMnScmWfmvRsmd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_016apivqhZ68q3ENYELBf11A","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/travis/build/failed/failed_reasons.txt","content":"# Build Failure Analysis for google/auto\n\n## Repository Information\n- **Repository**: google/auto\n- **Location**: /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto\n- **Project Type**: Maven multi-module project\n- **Description**: Google Auto - A collection of code generators for Java (AutoValue, AutoService, AutoFactory, AutoCommon)\n\n## Build Environment\n- **Java Version**: OpenJDK 11.0.27\n- **Build Tool**: Maven\n\n## Error Summary\n\n### Primary Error\n```\nCould not resolve dependencies for project X: Could not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 at specified path /usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n### Affected Modules\n1. **auto-common** (common/pom.xml)\n2. **auto-factory** (factory/pom.xml)\n3. **auto-service** (service/pom.xml)\n4. **auto-value** (value/pom.xml)\n\n## Root Cause Analysis\n\n### The Problem\nThe build fails because of a dependency on `com.sun:tools` (tools.jar) which no longer exists in Java 9+.\n\n### Technical Details\n1. In Java 8 and earlier, the JDK included `$JAVA_HOME/lib/tools.jar` containing:\n   - Java compiler API (com.sun.tools.javac.*)\n   - Annotation processing utilities\n   - Other development tools\n\n2. In Java 9+ (including Java 11 which is the current environment), `tools.jar` was removed as part of Project Jigsaw (JDK modularization). These classes are now part of:\n   - `java.compiler` module\n   - `jdk.compiler` module\n\n### Dependency Chain\nThe `com.sun:tools` dependency comes from the `compile-testing` library:\n\n```\nauto-common -> compile-testing:0.7 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-factory -> compile-testing:0.8 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-service -> compile-testing:0.6 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-value -> compile-testing:0.6 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\n```\n\nThe compile-testing library (versions 0.6, 0.7, 0.8) has a system-scoped dependency:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### Secondary Issue (auto-value only)\nThe auto-value module also has a dependency on `com.google.auto:auto-common:1.0-SNAPSHOT` which is not available in Maven Central (it's a snapshot version that needs to be built locally first).\n\n## Proposed Fix\n\n### Strategy\nUpdate the `compile-testing` dependency to version 0.18 which:\n1. In Java 8 and earlier, the JDK included `$JAVA_HOME/lib/tools.jar` containing:\n   - Java compiler API (com.sun.tools.javac.*)\n   - Annotation processing utilities\n   - Other development tools\n\n2. In Java 9+ (including Java 11 which is the current environment), `tools.jar` was removed as part of Project Jigsaw (JDK modularization). These classes are now part of:\n   - `java.compiler` module\n   - `jdk.compiler` module\n\n### Dependency Chain\nThe `com.sun:tools` dependency comes from the `compile-testing` library:\n\n```\nauto-common -> compile-testing:0.7 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-factory -> compile-testing:0.8 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-service -> compile-testing:0.6 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\nauto-value -> compile-testing:0.6 (test scope) -> com.sun:tools (system scope)\n```\n\nThe compile-testing library (versions 0.6, 0.7, 0.8) has a system-scoped dependency:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### Secondary Issue (auto-value only)\nThe auto-value module also has a dependency on `com.google.auto:auto-common:1.0-SNAPSHOT` which is not available in Maven Central (it's a snapshot version that needs to be built locally first).\n\n## Proposed Fix\n\n### Strategy\nUpdate the `compile-testing` dependency to version 0.18 which:\n1. Removed the `tools.jar` dependency\n2. Is compatible with Java 9, 11, 17, and newer\n3. Uses the standard javax.annotation.processing API\n\n### Files to Modify\n1. **common/pom.xml** (line 57): Update compile-testing from 0.7 to 0.18\n2. **factory/pom.xml** (line 74): Update compile-testing from 0.8 to 0.18\n3. **service/pom.xml** (line 57): Update compile-testing from 0.6 to 0.18\n4. **value/pom.xml** (line 82): Update compile-testing from 0.6 to 0.18\n5. **value/pom.xml** (line 49): Update auto-common from 1.0-SNAPSHOT to 0.10 (a released version)\n\n### Additional Changes Required\nThe compile-testing 0.18 has updated dependencies (truth version, guava version) that should be compatible with the existing codebase.\n\n## Secondary Error (Discovered After Initial Fix)\n\n### Error Message\n```\ncannot find symbol\n  symbol:   class Generated\n  location: package javax.annotation\n```\n\n### Affected Files\n1. factory/src/main/java/com/google/auto/factory/processor/FactoryWriter.java (line 39, 60)\n2. value/src/main/java/com/google/auto/value/processor/AutoAnnotationProcessor.java (line 36)\n3. value/src/main/java/com/google/auto/value/processor/AutoValueProcessor.java (line 51)\n\n### Root Cause\nIn Java 9, `javax.annotation.Generated` was moved to `javax.annotation.processing.Generated`.\nThe old `javax.annotation.Generated` was part of Java EE annotations that were deprecated in Java 9\nand removed in Java 11.\n\n### Additional Fix Required\nChange import from:\n```java\nimport javax.annotation.Generated;\n```\nTo:\n```java\nimport javax.annotation.processing.Generated;\n```\n\nAnd update the string literal in FactoryWriter.java line 60 from:\n```java\n.emitImports(\"javax.annotation.Generated\");\n```\nTo:\n```java\n.emitImports(\"javax.annotation.processing.Generated\");\n```\n\n## Error Type Classification\n- **Type**: Build Configuration Error + Code Compatibility Error\n- **Category**: Dependency Resolution / Java Version Compatibility\n- **Severity**: Build Breaking\n- **Fix Complexity**: Simple -
